# U3 gate: every item to close before the hand-back

The one list for the gate's fix round (roadmap U3, "The gate's fix round"). Every [B] and broken issue from the cold scores,
every [C] issue, and every open row of the audit, each tagged with its batch. An item is ticked here when its batch is reviewed and merged
into the working tree, with the test that proves it; work still in a helper's unmerged copy is not ticked. The re-score confirms it. Nits are in the score files only. Sources: `scores/` (two scorers, by screen), `audit-open-rows.md`.

## From the cold scores

### help-menu (G4)
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 5 Comp · `help-menu-day-1440.png`, y 60-190: the icons carry the wrong meaning: "Show keyboard shortcuts" uses a question-mark circle (the same glyph as the button that opens the menu), "Setup guide" uses a check mark (reads as "done" or as a selected item), "Missed something?" and "Write feedback" use the same speech-bubble outline. Fix: a keyboard icon, a list/compass icon for the guide, a flag or search icon for "Missed something?". → fixed (G1d): keyboard, book and flag icons, web/src/shell/Header.test.tsx "each Help entry has an icon of its own"; was: the guide and Missed icons are now book and search (not the repeated question mark or check), but the Show keyboard shortcuts item still uses the grid glyph because ui/Icon has no keyboard glyph; web/src/shell/Header.test.tsx (Help is a menu)
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 4 Type · `help-menu-day-1440.png`: "Missed something?" does not say what it does (it is a report that something was missed); "Version 0.1.0" is muted 13px, fine. Fix: label "Report something Reginald missed". → fixed: web/src/shell/Header.test.tsx (the missed-something item reads "Report something Helper missed")
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 8 Phone · no 390 picture of this menu: the phone's help items live in the More sheet (`more-sheet-day-390.png`), whose Help group is cut at the window bottom after two items ("Missed something?", "Show keyboard shortcuts"), so "Setup guide", "Write feedback" and the version cannot be seen without scrolling and no scroll cue shows. Fix: capture it; add a fade or scrollbar cue. → fixed: web/src/shell/BottomBar.test.tsx (More has the work screens… Help is the last group, with a fade cue at the sheet foot; capture more-sheet)
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 2 Layout · `help-menu-day-1440.png`: the menu (x 1145-1380) is placed 60px left of the window edge and does not align with the "?" button (x 1363) it hangs from; it covers the right edge of the setup banner's "..." menu. Fix: right-align the menu to the button. → judged wrong: the menu is within 6px of its button in the new capture help-menu-day-1440.png; nothing to align

### more-sheet (G4)
- [x] [G4] [B] (A-part2) 2 Layout · `more-sheet-day-390.png`, y 395-670: the "Saved items" group holds Personal, People, Files, Notes, Knowledge and Settings: Settings, People, Files, Notes and Knowledge are not saved items (the desktop sidebar keeps them as separate entries), and the four projects ("Harborview...", "Willowbrook...", "Northfield...", "Lantern Row...") that are saved items on desktop are missing here. The grouping is wrong. Fix: group as Keeping track / Projects / People and files / Settings, and list every project. → fixed: web/src/shell/BottomBar.test.tsx (More is Your work, Projects, People and files, Settings, Help; no "Saved items")
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 5 Comp · `more-sheet-day-390.png`, y 445: the "Personal" row has no icon, its text starts at x 24 while every other row starts at x 54 (icon column), so the list has two text edges. Fix: an icon (or initial tile like the desktop's "P") on every row. → fixed: web/src/shell/BottomBar.test.tsx (every row has an icon tile, Personal included)
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 5 Comp · `more-sheet-day-390.png`, y 760-805: "Missed something?" uses a warning triangle, which reads as an error, and "Show keyboard shortcuts" is a phone item for a keyboard that a phone rarely has. Fix: hide shortcuts below 600px; a neutral icon. → fixed: web/src/shell/BottomBar.test.tsx (no shortcuts item on the phone sheet; Report something missed has the search icon)
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 7 Edge · `more-sheet-day-390.png`, bottom (y 805-845): the sheet is cut at the window with the Help group half shown ("Setup guide", "Write feedback", version are not visible); no scroll cue. Fix: fade at the bottom edge and an inner scroll. → fixed: web/src/shell/BottomBar.test.tsx (the sheet scrolls with a fade cue; capture more-sheet-day-390.png)
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 8 Phone · `more-sheet-day-390.png`: rows are 44px apart, fine, but the sheet has a "More" title and a close x at the top right, 40px hit; the sheet does not have the tab bar visible behind it, so the person cannot switch directly to Today. OK. The handle is 40px wide, fine. → judged wrong: the scorer itself found the sheet fine (44px rows, 40px hit, handle 40px); nothing to change

### search-results (G2)

- [x] [G2] [C] (A-part2) 5 Comp · `search-results-day-390.png`: result type tags "Person" and "To-do" are plain right-aligned text with no pill, while `todo-page-day-1440.png` uses boxed tags for the same words. Fix: use the one tag style. → fixed: `web/src/components/SearchBox.test.tsx::each result has a glyph and its kind as a tag, and Asking is set apart from the results by a rule and its own glyph` (one panel, scrim on a phone)
- [x] [G2] [C] (A-part2) 7 Edge · no picture of no results, of many results, or of a long name. Fix: capture those. → fixed: see g2a-capture
- [x] [G2] [C] (A-part2) 2 Layout · `search-results-day-390.png`: the popover is 365px wide, full-width on the phone, with its top attached to the field; but the header's Reginald status chip and the search icon vanish while search is open, and the page below shows the Today screen cut by the popover (y 330). ### shortcuts (G4)

The key column's descriptions start at x 364 for all, good. Fix: 18px title, 13px rows, 12px keys with padding. → fixed: web/src/components/ScheduleReview.test.tsx (the shortcuts list is three labeled groups) and capture shortcuts-day-1440.png (title in the display face over 13px rows; keys 12px with padding, ModalPanel.module.css, ShortcutHelp.module.css)
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 5 Comp · `shortcuts-day-1440.png`, y 368: "Enter" and "Esc" chips are wider than "j" and "k" so the key column has a ragged right edge; the "Swipe" row at y 521 is a gesture, not a key, and "On a phone, swipe sideways to move" is in a keyboard dialog. Fix: fixed-width key cells; move Swipe out. → fixed: web/src/components/ScheduleReview.test.tsx (the shortcuts list is three labeled groups) (keys in cells of one width; the swipe row is gone)
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 8 Phone · no 390 picture: a shortcuts dialog on a phone is not useful (the More sheet still offers "Show keyboard shortcuts"). Fix: hide the command below 600px, or capture it. → fixed: web/src/shell/BottomBar.test.tsx (the phone sheet has no shortcuts item, so no shortcuts dialog is reachable on a phone)
- [x] [G4] [C] (A-part2) 7 Edge · no state picture; the list is longer than the 625px 1440 window, the dialog ends at y 572 with its Close button, so nothing scrolls here, but at 1024x768 and smaller windows height may cut it (1024 not opened; check). → fixed: the dialog scrolls inside the window by the shared Overlay (max-height 100dvh - 32px) and its rows are compact; capture shortcuts-day-1024.png

### status-popover (G1)
- [x] [G1] [B] (A-part2) 4 Type · `status-popover-day-1440.png`, y 120-195: the Connections summary is a single run-on paragraph of 11px text mixing roman and italic: "Checked 2 connections, last at 9:33 PM · Mail from before Tue, Oct 20 has not been read yet · Your inbox, avery.lane.home@example.com not read yet · Your calendar, avery.lane.home@example.com not read yet · Your inbox, avery.lane.studio@example.com not read yet · Your calendar, ...". It repeats what the three account rows directly below say (Read 9:33 PM / Not read yet). Fix: delete the paragraph; keep the account rows, and show one line "Checked 9:33 PM · mail before Oct 20 not read yet". → **fixed in G1 pass 2, merged: StatusMenu.test.tsx (one column, urgent first, one row pattern, sheet title and close).**
- [x] [G1] [B] (A-part2) 2 Layout · `status-popover-day-1440.png`, y 35-495: the popover is 445px wide and 460px tall on a 625px window and covers the page's greeting and the first list; it has five sections (Updates, Backup, Connections, Restart, Reginald) in two columns with unequal heights, plus a nested list of accounts with its own card and a full-width "Open Connections" button. Rubric: no card inside a card. Fix: a single column, four rows (status dot, label, one action), accounts folded under Connections. → **fixed in G1 pass 2, merged: StatusMenu.test.tsx (one column, urgent first, one row pattern, sheet title and close).**
- [x] [G1] [B] (A-part2) 1 Hier · `status-popover-day-1440.png`: three actions look alike: "Refresh" (outline), "Set up backups" (outline), "Open AI account setup" (outline) and one filled "Restart now"; the Restart row is the one that blocks work but sits at the bottom left, under the long paragraph. Fix: order rows by urgency: Restart first. → **fixed in G1 pass 2, merged: StatusMenu.test.tsx (one column, urgent first, one row pattern, sheet title and close).**
- [x] [G1] [C] (A-part2) 8 Phone · `status-popover-day-390.png`: the popover is a full-width sheet whose top is under the header (y 47), no visible title or close, 800px+ tall; the 12-line italic paragraph is at 17px and pushes accounts to y 480. "Reginald" section is cut at the bottom (y 840). Fix: the paragraph fix above; sheet with a title and a close. → **fixed in G1 pass 2, merged: StatusMenu.test.tsx (one column, urgent first, one row pattern, sheet title and close).**
- [x] [G1] [C] (A-part2) 5 Comp · `status-popover-day-1440.png`: colours of the three status words (brown "No backup on another device", brown "Restart the app...", brown "The assistant isn't set up yet") are all the same amber with no icon or dot; the header chip beside it is "Restart to finish" in a different style from the "Reginald" with a triangle. Fix: one status row pattern (dot or icon, word, action). → **fixed in G1 pass 2, merged: StatusMenu.test.tsx (one column, urgent first, one row pattern, sheet title and close).**
- [ ] [G1] [C] (A-part2) 6 Colour · `status-popover-day-1440.png`: status brown on white is about 5:1; ok. `status-popover-night-1440.png` has orange on dark at 8:1; ok. The state is by text plus colour; the dots on the account rows are green/grey by colour with words beside them, ok. → **judged wrong by G1: the line says "ok" and asks for nothing.**
- [x] [G1] [C] (A-part2) 3 Space · `status-popover-day-1440.png`: section gaps vary (Updates to Connections 12px, Connections to Restart 24px, rows inside accounts 0 with hairlines); labels at 10px uppercase-free sentence case differ from the page's uppercase group labels. → **fixed in G1 pass 2, merged: StatusMenu.test.tsx (one column, urgent first, one row pattern, sheet title and close).**

## CI failures found during the round (batch T1, test robustness)

From CI on f15e5e6, a docs-only commit whose code passed on c83736d. The web job ran from 23:55 to 00:12 UTC, across midnight.
- [ ] [T1] **The journeys cross midnight.** `journeys-mail.spec.ts:187` (J2) and `journeys-new-start.spec.ts:292` (J6) don't find "Venue walk-through" on Today's Schedule. `tests/fake_services.py` `events()` places the near events from the server's start time, so they stay clear of midnight at that moment. But the test reaches Today minutes later, after midnight, and by then the events are on yesterday. Fix: the e2e servers and the browser share one fixed clock for the journeys (a fixed time of day, set in the test process with `time-machine` and the page clock), not the real time. Never faketime.
- [ ] [T1] **`side-filter.spec.ts:72` and `:136`**, a calendar's clash and meetings under their side. Check whether the same midnight cause applies, then fix it with the same clock.
- [ ] [T1] **`settings-walk.spec.ts:273`, the search on Connections and Add-ons.** It failed again after the fix in c83736d, so the cause isn't settled. Reproduce it locally under load, find the race, and fix it in the screen or the test's wait, never by loosening the assertion.

## From the cold score of G3's Schedule screens (scorer D, `scores/D-g3.md`)

15 of 28 screens and states pass 4.0. Of the two mockup pages, `schedule` scores 4.06 and `schedule-states` 3.88, against the 4.6 bar. The shared parts (header, drawer, offline bar, skeleton, disabled button, empty card) are G1b; the rest is G3c.

## From the cold score of G2a's screens (scorer E, `scores/E-g2a.md`)

6 of 9 pass 4.0. `today` and `today-states` score 4.2 against the 4.6 mockup bar (brand 4.5). The loading nav shift is G1b (in its brief); everything else is G2c: the three capture faults, today-review, what-changed, stages, and Today to the mockup at 4.6.

## Found by the fix of the merged run

- [ ] [T1] **`quick-edits.spec.ts` "three Resets" may be a real race, not load** (the P0 X01 area: quick edits lost). It failed twice in loaded runs: all three saves were answered, yet after the reload the month value was not the default. Reproduce it with the saves forced close together. Find the race in the save queue or the reset path, and fix it with a unit test.
- [ ] [T1] **`journeys.spec.ts` J7's toast waits were raised to 20s** after it failed in three loaded runs. Find what it waits on and wait on that condition, not on time.

## Found while reviewing G4c

- [ ] [G4d] **"Files from <person>" never shows, because no file is linked to a person.** G4c reads `meta.person`, which nothing sets. Link a file to the person it came from in the engine: a mail attachment, or a hand-in naming them, records the sender's person id where the file's source is recorded. Then the card and the tab read that field, not an ad-hoc meta key. Needs a Python test, the API model, and a capture stage with such a file.
- [ ] [G4d] **The person mockup comparison never shows "Draft a message" or the Files card:** the capture workspace has nothing waiting on the person and no file from them. Add a capture stage with both, so the person and person-states side-by-sides can be scored against the mockup.
- [x] [G1d] **At 390 the person page's four tabs wrap onto two rows.** Shared tabs scroll in one row on a phone, or fold into a "More" menu. → fixed: web/src/ui/sharedLook.test.tsx "a phone's tabs are one row that scrolls inside its own box" (gate-fix/g1d-capture2/person-page-day-390.png: one row); the layout checks (web/e2e/layout.spec.ts, handled.spec.ts, journey-helpers.ts) now skip what sits inside a box that scrolls
